tathā pañ‍ñāpāramiṃ sampādaitukāmaina yasmā pañ‍ñā ālaukau viya andhakāraina mauhaina saha na vattati, tasmā mauhakāraṇāni tāva baudhisattaina parivaj‍jaitabbāni| tatthimāni  mauhakāraṇāni – arati tandi vijambhitā ālasiyaṃ gaṇasaṅgaṇikārāmatā niddāsīlatā anicchayasīlatā ñāṇasmiṃ akutūhalatā micchādhimānau aparipucchakatā kāyassa na sammāparihārau  asamāhitacittatā duppañ‍ñānaṃ puggalānaṃ saivanā pañ‍ñavantānaṃ apayirupāsanā attaparibhavau micchāvikappau viparītābhinivaisau kāyadaḷhibahulatā asaṃvaigasīlatā pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni| saṅkhaipatau yai vā pana dhammai āsaivatau anuppan‍nā pañ‍ñā nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nā parihāyati| iti imāni sammauhakāraṇāni parivaj‍jaintaina bāhusac‍cai jhānādīsu ca yaugau karaṇīyau|

tatthāyaṃ bāhusac‍cassa visayavibhāgau – pañ‍cakkhandhā dvādasāyatanāni aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau cattāri sac‍cāni bāvīsatindriyāni dvādasapadikau paṭic‍casamuppādau tathā satipaṭṭhānādayau kusalādidhammappakārabhaidā ca, yāni ca laukai anavaj‍jāni vij‍jāṭṭhānāni, yai ca sattānaṃ hitasukhavidhānayauggā  byākaraṇavisaisā, iti aivaṃ pakāraṃ sakalamaiva sutavisayaṃ upāyakausal‍lapubbaṅgamāya pañ‍ñāya satiyā vīriyaina ca sādhukaṃ uggahaṇasavanadhāraṇaparicayaparipucchāhi augāhaitvā tattha ca paraisaṃ patiṭṭhāpanaina sutamayā pañ‍ñā nibbattaitabbā|

tathā sattānaṃ itikattabbatāsu ṭhānuppattikapaṭibhānabhūtā āyāpāyaupāyakausal‍labhūtā ca pañ‍ñā hitaisitaṃ nissāya tattha tattha yathārahaṃ pavattaitabbā| tathā khandhādīnaṃ sabhāvadhammānaṃ ākāraparivitak‍kanamukhaina tai nijjhānaṃ khamāpaintaina cintāmayā pañ‍ñā nibbattaitabbā| khandhādīnaṃyaiva pana salakkhaṇasāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇapariggahaṇavasaina laukiyapariñ‍ñā nibbattaintaina pubbabhāgabhāvanāpañ‍ñā sampādaitabbā| aivañhi nāmarūpamattamidaṃ yathārahaṃ pac‍cayaihi uppaj‍jati caiva nirujjhati ca, na aittha kauci kattā vā kāraitā vā, hutvā abhāvaṭṭhaina anic‍caṃ, udayabbayapaṭipīḷanaṭṭhaina dukkhaṃ, avasavattanaṭṭhaina anattāti ajjhattikadhammai bāhirakadhammai ca nibbisaisaṃ parijānantau tattha āsaṅgaṃ pajahantau parai ca tattha taṃ jahāpaintau kaivalaṃ karuṇāvasainaiva yāva na buddhaguṇā  hatthatalaṃ āgacchanti, tāva yānattayai sattai avatāraṇaparipācanaihi patiṭṭhapaintau jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattiyau abhiñ‍ñāyau ca laukiyā vasībhāvaṃ pāpaintau pañ‍ñāya matthakaṃ pāpuṇāti|

tattha yā imā iddhividhañāṇaṃ dibbasautadhātuñāṇaṃ caitaupariyañāṇaṃ pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ yathākammupagañāṇaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇanti saparibhaṇḍā pañ‍calaukiyaabhiñ‍ñāsaṅkhātā bhāvanāpañ‍ñā, yā ca khandhāyatanadhātuindriyasac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādādibhaidaisu bhūmibhūtaisu dhammaisu uggahaparipucchāvasaina ñāṇaparicayaṃ katvā sīlavisuddhicittavisuddhīti  mūlabhūtāsu imāsu dvīsu visuddhīsu patiṭṭhāya diṭṭhivisuddhikaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhimaggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhi- paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiñāṇadassanavisuddhīti sarīrabhūtā imā pañ‍ca visuddhiyau sampādaintaina bhāvaitabbā laukiyalaukuttarabhaidā bhāvanāpañ‍ñā, tāsaṃ sampādanavidhānaṃ yasmā ‘‘tattha aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hautītiādikaṃ iddhivikubbanaṃ kātukāmaina ādikammikaina yauginā’’tiādinā ‘‘khandhāti rūpakkhandhau vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau’’tiādinā ca visayavibhāgaina saddhiṃ  visuddhimaggai (visuddhi॰ 2.430 ādayau) sabbākāratau vitthāraitvā vuttaṃ, tasmā tattha vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| kaivalañhi tattha sāvakabaudhisattassa vasaina pañ‍ñā āgatā, idha mahābaudhisattassa vasaina karuṇūpāyakausal‍lapubbaṅgamaṃ katvā vattabbā, ñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ apāpaitvā paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiyaṃyaiva vipassanā ṭhapaitabbāti ayamaiva visaisau| aivamaittha pañ‍ñāpāramiyā paṭipattik‍kamau vaiditabbau|

tathā yasmā sammāsambaudhiyā katābhinīhāraina mahāsattaina pāramiparipūraṇatthaṃ sabbakālaṃ yuttappayuttaina bhavitabbaṃ ābaddhaparikaraṇaina, tasmā kālaina kālaṃ ‘‘kau nu khau aj‍ja mayā puñ‍ñasambhārau ñāṇasambhārau vā upacitau, kiṃ vā mayā parahitaṃ kata’’nti divasai divasai pac‍cavaikkhantaina sattahitatthaṃ ussāhau karaṇīyau| sabbaisampi sattānaṃ upakārāya attanau pariggahabhūtaṃ vatthu kāyai jīvitai ca nirapaikkhacittaina aussajitabbaṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci kammaṃ karauti kāyaina vācāya vā, taṃ sabbaṃ sambaudhiyaṃ  nin‍nacittainaiva kātabbaṃ, baudhiyā pariṇāmaitabbaṃ| uḷāraihi ittaraihi ca kāmaihi vinivattacittainaiva bhavitabbaṃ| sabbāsupi itikattabbatāsu upāyakausal‍laṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaitvāva paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

tasmiṃ tasmiṃ sattahitai āraddhavīriyaina bhavitabbaṃ iṭṭhāniṭṭhādisabbasahaina avisaṃvādinā| sabbaipi sattā anaudhisau maittāya karuṇāya ca pharitabbā| yā kāci sattānaṃ dukkhuppatti, sabbā sā attani pāṭikaṅkhitabbā| sabbaisañ‍ca sattānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ abbhanumauditabbaṃ| buddhānaṃ mahantatā mahānubhāvatā abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā| yañ‍ca kiñ‍ci kammaṃ karauti kāyaina vācāya vā, taṃ sabbaṃ baudhinin‍nacittapubbaṅgamaṃ kātabbaṃ| iminā hi upāyaina dānādīsu yuttappayuttassa thāmavatau daḷhaparak‍kamassa mahāsattassa baudhisattassa aparimaiyyau puñ‍ñasambhārau ñāṇasambhārau ca divasai divasai upacīyati|

api ca sattānaṃ paribhaugatthaṃ paripālanatthañ‍ca attanau sarīraṃ jīvitañ‍ca paric‍cajitvā khuppipāsāsītuṇhavātātapādidukkhapatikārau  pariyaisitabbau upanaitabbau ca| yañ‍ca yathāvuttadukkhapatikārajaṃ sukhaṃ attanā paṭilabhati, tathā ramaṇīyaisu ārāmuyyānapāsādataḷākādīsu arañ‍ñāyatanaisu ca kāyacittasantāpābhāvaina abhinibbutattā attanā  sukhaṃ paṭilabhati, yañ‍ca suṇāti buddhānubuddhapac‍caikabuddhā mahābaudhisattā ca naikkhammapaṭipattiyaṃ ṭhitā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārabhūtaṃ īdisaṃ nāma jhānasamāpattisukhaṃ anubhavantīti, taṃ sabbaṃ sabbasattaisu anaudhisau upasaṃharatīti| ayaṃ tāva nayau asamāhitabhūmiyaṃ patiṭṭhitassa|

samāhitabhūmiyaṃ pana patiṭṭhitau attanā yathānubhūtaṃ visaisādhigamanibbattaṃ pītiṃ passaddhiṃ sukhaṃ samādhiṃ yathābhūtañāṇañ‍ca sattaisu adhimuc‍cantau upasaṃharati pariṇāmaiti| tathā mahati saṃsāradukkhai tassa ca nimittabhūtai kilaisābhisaṅkhāradukkhai nimuggaṃ sattanikāyaṃ disvā tatrāpi chaidanabhaidanaphālanapiṃsanaggisantāpādijanitā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā nirantaraṃ cirakālaṃ vaidiyantai nārakai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kujjhanasantāpanavihaiṭhanahiṃsanaparādhīnatādīhi mahādukkhaṃ anubhavantai tiracchānagatai, jautimālākulasarīrai khuppipāsāvātātapādīhi ḍayhamānai  ca visussamānai ca vantakhaiḷādiāhārai uddhabāhuṃ viravantai nijjhāmataṇhikādikai mahādukkhaṃ vaidiyamānai paitai ca, pariyaiṭṭhimūlakaṃ mahantaṃ anayabyasanaṃ pāpuṇantai hatthacchaidādikāraṇāyaugaina dubbaṇṇaduddasikadaliddādibhāvaina khuppipāsādiābādhayaugaina balavantaihi abhibhavanīyatau paraisaṃ vahanatau parādhīnatau ca, nārakai paitai tiracchānagatai ca, atisayantai apāyadukkhanibbisaisaṃ dukkhamanubhavantai manussai ca, tathā visayavisaparibhaugavikkhittacittatāya rāgādipariḷāhaina ḍayhamānai vātavaigasamuṭṭhitajālāsamiddhasukkhakaṭṭhasan‍nipātai aggikkhandhai viya anupasantapariḷāhavuttikai anupasantanihataparādhīnai kāmāvacaradaivai ca, mahatā vāyāmaina vidūramākāsaṃ vigāhitasakuntā viya balavatā dūrai pāṇinā khittasarā viya ca, satipi cirappavattiyaṃ anic‍cantikatāya pātapariyausānā anatik‍kantajātijarāmaraṇā aivāti rūpārūpāvacaradaivai ca passantaina mahantaṃ saṃvaigaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhapaitvā maittāya karuṇāya ca anaudhisau sattā pharitabbā| aivaṃ kāyaina vācāya manasā ca baudhisambhārai nirantaraṃ upacinantaina yathā pāramiyau pāripūriṃ gacchanti, aivaṃ sak‍kac‍cakārinā sātac‍cakārinā anaulīnavuttinā ussāhau pavattaitabbau, vīriyapāramī paripūraitabbā|

api  ca acintaiyyāparimaiyyavipulauḷāravimalanirupamanirūpak‍kilaisaguṇanicayanidhānabhūtassa buddhabhāvassa ussak‍kitvā sampahaṃsanayauggaṃ vīriyaṃ nāma acintaiyyānubhāvamaiva, yaṃ na pacurajanā  sautumpi sak‍kuṇanti, pagaiva paṭipaj‍jituṃ| tathā hi tividhā abhinīhāracittuppatti, catassau buddhabhūmiyau, cattāri saṅgahavatthūni, karuṇaikarasatā, buddhadhammaisu sacchikaraṇaina visaisappac‍cayau nijjhānakhanti, sabbadhammaisu nirupalaipau, sabbasattaisu piyaputtasañ‍ñā, saṃsāradukkhaihi aparikhaidau, sabbadaiyyadhammaparic‍cāgau, taina ca niratimānatā, adhisīlādiadhiṭṭhānaṃ, tattha ca acañ‍calatā, kusalakiriyāsu pītipāmauj‍jaṃ, vivaikanin‍nacittatā, jhānānuyaugau, anavaj‍jadhammaina atitti, yathāsutassa dhammassa paraisaṃ hitajjhāsayaina daisanā, sattānaṃ ñāyai nivaisanā, ārambhadaḷhatā, dhīravīrabhāvau, parāpavādaparāpakāraisu vikārābhāvau, sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ, samāpattīsu  vasībhāvau, abhiñ‍ñāsu balappatti, lakkhaṇattayāvabaudhau, satipaṭṭhānādīsu yaugakammābhiyaugaina laukuttaramaggasambhārasambharaṇaṃ, navalaukuttarāvak‍kantīti aivamādikā sabbāpi baudhisambhārapaṭipatti vīriyānubhāvainaiva samijjhatīti abhinīhāratau yāva mahābaudhi anaussaj‍jantaina sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ vīriyaṃ yathā uparūpari visaisāvahaṃ hauti, aivaṃ sampādaitabbaṃ| sampaj‍jamānai ca yathāvuttai vīriyai khantisac‍cādhiṭṭhānādayau ca dānasīlādayau ca sabbaipi baudhisambhārā tadadhīnavuttitāya sampan‍nā aiva hauntīti khantiādīsupi imināva nayaina paṭipatti vaiditabbā|

iti sattānaṃ sukhūpakaraṇaparic‍cāgaina bahudhā anuggahakaraṇaṃ dānaina paṭipatti, sīlaina taisaṃ jīvitasāpataiyyadārarakkhāabhaidapiyahitavacanāvihiṃsādikāraṇāni, naikkhammaina taisaṃ āmisapaṭiggahaṇadhammadānādinā anaikavidhā hitacariyā, pañ‍ñāya taisaṃ hitakaraṇūpāyakausal‍laṃ, vīriyaina tattha ussāhārambhaasaṃhīrāni, khantiyā tadaparādhasahanaṃ, sac‍caina naisaṃ avañ‍canatadupakārakiriyāsamādānāvisaṃvādanādi, adhiṭṭhānaina tadupakārakaraṇai anatthasampātaipi acalanaṃ, maittāya naisaṃ hitasukhānucintanaṃ, upaikkhāya naisaṃ upakārāpakāraisu vikārānāpattīti aivaṃ aparimāṇai sattai ārabbha anukampitasabbasattassa mahābaudhisattassa  puthuj‍janaihi asādhāraṇau aparimaiyyau puñ‍ñañāṇasambhārūpacayau aittha paṭipattīti vaiditabbaṃ| yau caitāsaṃ pac‍cayau vuttau, tassa ca sak‍kac‍caṃ sampādanaṃ|

kau vibhāgauti? dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samattiṃsapāramiyau| tattha katābhinīhārassa baudhisattassa parahitakaraṇābhinin‍nāsayapayaugassa kaṇhadhammavaukiṇṇā suk‍kadhammā pāramiyau aiva, taihi avaukiṇṇā suk‍kadhammā upapāramiyau, akaṇhaasuk‍kā paramatthapāramiyauti kaici| samudāgamanakālaisu vā pūriyamānā pāramiyau, baudhisattabhūmiyaṃ puṇṇā upapāramiyau, buddhabhūmiyaṃ sabbākāraparipuṇṇā paramatthapāramiyau| baudhisattabhūmiyaṃ  vā parahitakaraṇatau pāramiyau, attahitakaraṇatau upapāramiyau, buddhabhūmiyaṃ balavaisāraj‍jasamadhigamaina ubhayahitaparipūraṇatau paramatthapāramiyau|

aivaṃ ādimajjhapariyausānaisu paṇidhānārambhapariniṭṭhānaisu taisaṃ vibhāgauti aparai| dausūpasamakaruṇāpakatikānaṃ bhavasukhavimuttisukhaparamasukhappattānaṃ  puñ‍ñūpacayabhaidatau tabbibhāgauti añ‍ñai| laj‍jāsatimānāpassayānaṃ laukuttaradhammādhipatīnaṃ sīlasamādhipañ‍ñāgarukānaṃ tāritataritatārayitūnaṃ anubuddhapac‍caikabuddhasammāsambuddhānaṃ pāramī, upapāramī, paramatthapāramīti baudhisattassuppattitau yathāvuttavibhāgauti kaici| cittapaṇidhitau yāva vacīpaṇidhi, tāva pavattā sambhārā pāramiyau, vacīpaṇidhitau yāva kāyapaṇidhi, tāva pavattā upapāramiyau, kāyapaṇidhitau pabhuti paramatthapāramiyauti aparai| añ‍ñai pana ‘‘parapuñ‍ñānumaudanavasaina pavattā sambhārā pāramiyau, paraisaṃ kārāpanavasaina pavattā upapāramiyau, sayaṃkaraṇavasaina pavattā paramatthapāramiyau’’ti vadanti|

tathā bhavasukhāvahau puñ‍ñañāṇasambhārau pāramī, attanau nibbānasukhāvahau upapāramī, paraisaṃ tadubhayasukhāvahau paramatthapāramīti aikai| puttadāradhanādiupakaraṇaparic‍cāgau pana dānapāramī, aṅgaparic‍cāgau dānaupapāramī, attanau jīvitaparic‍cāgau dānaparamatthapāramīti| tathā puttadārādikassa tividhassāpi haitu avītik‍kamanavasaina tissau sīlapāramiyau, taisu aiva tividhaisu vatthūsu ālayaṃ upacchinditvā nikkhamanavasaina  tissau naikkhammapāramiyau, upakaraṇaṅgajīvitataṇhaṃ samūhanitvā sattānaṃ hitāhitavinicchayakaraṇavasaina tissau pañ‍ñāpāramiyau| yathāvuttabhaidānaṃ paric‍cāgādīnaṃ vāyamanavasaina tissau vīriyapāramiyau, upakaraṇaṅgajīvitantarāyakarānaṃ khamanavasaina tissau khantipāramiyau, upakaraṇaṅgajīvitahaitu sac‍cāparic‍cāgavasaina tissau sac‍capāramiyau, dānādipāramiyau akuppādhiṭṭhānavasainaiva samijjhantīti, upakaraṇādivināsaipi acalādhiṭṭhānavasaina tissau adhiṭṭhānapāramiyau, upakaraṇādiupaghātakaisupi sattaisu maittāya avijahanavasaina tissau maittāpāramiyau, yathāvuttavatthuttayassa upakārāpakāraisu sattasaṅkhāraisu majjhattatāpaṭilābhavasaina tissau upaikkhāpāramiyauti aivamādinā aitāsaṃ vibhāgau vaiditabbāti|

kau saṅgahauti? aittha pana yathā aitā vibhāgatau tiṃsavidhāpi dānapāramiādibhāvatau dasavidhā, aivaṃ dānasīlakhantivīriyajhānapañ‍ñāsabhāvaina chabbidhā| aitāsu hi naikkhammapāramī sīlapāramiyā saṅgahitā, tassā pabbaj‍jābhāvai, nīvaraṇavivaikabhāvai pana jhānapāramiyā kusaladhammabhāvai  chahipi saṅgahitā| sac‍capāramī sīlapāramiyā  aikadaisau aiva vacīviratisac‍capakkhai, ñāṇasac‍capakkhai pana pañ‍ñāpāramiyā saṅgahitā| maittāpārami jhānapāramiyā aiva| upaikkhāpāramī jhānapañ‍ñāpāramīhi| adhiṭṭhānapāramī sabbāhipi saṅgahitāti|

aitaisañ‍ca dānādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ guṇānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñasambandhānaṃ pañ‍cadasayugalādīni pañ‍cadasayugalādisādhakāni haunti| saiyyathidaṃ – dānasīlayugalaina parahitāhitānaṃ karaṇākaraṇayugalasiddhi, dānakhantiyugalaina alaubhādausayugalasiddhi, dānavīriyayugalaina cāgasutayugalasiddhi, dānajhānayugalaina kāmadausappahānayugalasiddhi| dānapañ‍ñāyugalaina ariyayānadhurayugalasiddhi, sīlakhantidvayaina payaugāsayasuddhidvayasiddhi, sīlavīriyadvayaina bhāvanādvayasiddhi, sīlajhānadvayaina dussīlyapariyuṭṭhānappahānadvayasiddhi, sīlapañ‍ñādvayaina dānadvayasiddhi, khantivīriyayugalaina khamātaijadvayasiddhi, khantijhānayugalaina viraudhānuraudhappahānayugalasiddhi, khantipañ‍ñāyugalaina suñ‍ñatākhantipaṭivaidhadukasiddhi, vīriyajhānadukaina paggahāvikkhaipadukasiddhi, vīriyapañ‍ñādukaina saraṇadukasiddhi, jhānapañ‍ñādukaina yānadukasiddhi, dānasīlakhantittikaina laubhadausamauhappahānattikasiddhi, dānasīlavīriyattikaina bhaugajīvitakāyasārādānattikasiddhi, dānasīlajhānattikaina puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuttikasiddhi, dānasīlapañ‍ñātikaina āmisābhayadhammadānattikasiddhīti| aivaṃ itaraihipi tikaihi catuk‍kādīhi ca yathāsambhavaṃ tikāni ca catuk‍kādīni ca yaujaitabbāni|

aivaṃ chabbidhānampi pana imāsaṃ pāramīnaṃ catūhi adhiṭṭhānaihi saṅgahau vaiditabbau| sabbapāramīnaṃ samūhasaṅgahatau hi cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ, cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ, upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānanti| tattha adhitiṭṭhati aitaina, aittha vā adhitiṭṭhati, adhiṭṭhānamattamaiva vā tanti adhiṭṭhānaṃ, sac‍cañ‍ca taṃ adhiṭṭhānañ‍ca, sac‍cassa vā adhiṭṭhānaṃ, sac‍caṃ adhiṭṭhānamaitassāti vā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ| aivaṃ saisaisupi| tattha avisaisatau tāva laukuttaraguṇai katābhinīhārassa anukampitasabbasattassa mahāsattassa paṭiñ‍ñānurūpaṃ sabbapāramipariggahatau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ| tāsaṃ paṭipakkhaparic‍cāgatau cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ| sabbapāramitāguṇaihi upasamatau upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ| taihi aiva parahitaupāyakausal‍latau pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

visaisatau pana yācakajanaṃ avisaṃvādaitvā dassāmīti paṭijānanatau paṭiñ‍ñaṃ avisaṃvādaitvā  dānatau dānaṃ avisaṃvādaitvā anumaudanatau macchariyādipaṭipakkhaparic‍cāgatau daiyyadhammapaṭiggāhakadānadaiyyadhammakkhayaisu laubhadausamauhabhayavūpasamatau yathārahaṃ yathākālaṃ yathāvidhānañ‍ca dānatau pañ‍ñuttaratau ca kusaladhammānaṃ caturadhiṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānaṃ dānaṃ| tathā saṃvarasamādānassa avītik‍kamanatau dussīlyaparic‍cāgatau duc‍caritavūpasamanatau pañ‍ñuttaratau ca caturadhiṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānaṃ sīlaṃ| yathāpaṭiñ‍ñaṃ khamanatau, parāparādhavikappaparic‍cāgatau, kaudhapariyuṭṭhānavūpasamanatau, pañ‍ñuttaratau, ca caturadhiṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānā khanti| paṭiñ‍ñānurūpaṃ parahitakaraṇatau, visadaparic‍cāgatau, akusalavūpasamanatau, pañ‍ñuttaratau ca caturadhiṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānaṃ vīriyaṃ| paṭiñ‍ñānurūpaṃ laukahitānucintanatau, nīvaraṇaparic‍cāgatau, cittavūpasamanatau, pañ‍ñuttaratau ca caturadhiṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānaṃ jhānaṃ| yathāpaṭiñ‍ñaṃ parahitūpāyakausal‍latau, anupāyakiriyāparic‍cāgatau, mauhajapariḷāhavūpasamanatau, sabbañ‍ñutāpaṭilābhatau ca caturadhiṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānā pañ‍ñā|

tattha  ñaiyyapaṭiñ‍ñānuvidhānaihi sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ| vatthukāmakilaisakāmaparic‍cāgaihi cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ| dausadukkhavūpasamaihi upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ| anubaudhapaṭivaidhaihi pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ| tividhasac‍capariggahitaṃ dausattayaviraudhi sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ| tividhacāgapariggahitaṃ dausattayaviraudhi cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ| tividhavūpasamapariggahitaṃ dausattayaviraudhi upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ| tividhañāṇapariggahitaṃ dausattayaviraudhi pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ| sac‍cādhiṭṭhānapariggahitāni cāgūpasamapañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānāni avisaṃvādanatau paṭiñ‍ñānuvidhānatau ca, cāgādhiṭṭhānapariggahitāni sac‍cūpasamapañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānāni paṭipakkhaparic‍cāgatau sabbaparic‍cāgaphalattā ca, upasamādhiṭṭhānapariggahitāni sac‍cacāgapañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānāni kilaisapariḷāhavūpasamanatau kāmūpasamanatau kāmapariḷāhavūpasamanatau ca, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānapariggahitāni sac‍cacāgūpasamādhiṭṭhānāni ñāṇapubbaṅgamatau ñāṇānuparivattanatau cāti aivaṃ sabbāpi pāramiyau sac‍cappabhāvitā cāgaparibyañ‍jitā upasamaupabrūhitā  pañ‍ñāparisuddhā| sac‍cañhi aitāsaṃ janakahaitu| cāgau pariggāhakahaitu, upasamau parivuddhihaitu, pañ‍ñā pārisuddhihaitu| tathā ādimhi sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ sac‍capaṭiñ‍ñattā, majjhai cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ katapaṇidhānassa parahitāya attaparic‍cāgatau| antai upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ sabbūpasamapariyausānattā| ādimajjhapariyausānaisu pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ tasmiṃ sati sambhavatau asati abhāvatau yathāpaṭiñ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvatau|

tattha mahāpurisā satatamattahitaparahitakaraihi garupiyabhāvakaraihi sac‍cacāgādhiṭṭhānaihi gihibhūtā  āmisadānaina parai anuggaṇhanti| tathā attahitaparahitakaraihi garupiyabhāvakaraihi upasamapañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaihi ca pabbajitabhūtā dhammadānaina parai anuggaṇhanti|

tattha antimabhavai baudhisattassa caturadhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaṃ| paripuṇṇacaturadhiṭṭhānassa hi carimakabhavūpapattīti aikai| tatra hi gabbhauk‍kantiṭhitiabhinikkhamanaisu pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina satau sampajānau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānapāripūriyā sampatijātau uttarābhimukhau sattapadavītihāraina gantvā sabbā disā aulaukaitvā sac‍cānuparivattinā vacasā – ‘‘aggauhamasmi laukassa, jaiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassa, saiṭṭhauhamasmi laukassā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.31; ma॰ ni॰ 3.207) tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadi|

upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina  jiṇṇāturamatapabbajitadassāvinau catudhammapadaisakauvidassa yaubbanāraugyajīvitasampattimadānaṃ upasamau| cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina mahatau ñātiparivaṭṭassa hatthagatassa ca cak‍kavattiraj‍jassa anapaikkhaparic‍cāgauti|

dutiyai ṭhānai abhisambaudhiyaṃ caturadhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇanti kaici| tattha hi yathāpaṭiñ‍ñaṃ sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ abhisamayau, tatau hi sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina sabbakilaisūpak‍kilaisaparic‍cāgau, tatau hi cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina paramūpasamappatti, tatau hi upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina anāvaraṇañāṇapaṭilābhau, tatau hi pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇanti| taṃ asiddhaṃ, abhisambaudhiyāpi paramatthabhāvatau|

tatiyai ṭhānai dhammacak‍kappavattanai caturadhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇanti añ‍ñai| tattha hi sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa dvādasahi ākāraihi ariyasac‍cadaisanāya  sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa saddhammamahāyāgakaraṇaina cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa sayaṃ upasantassa paraisaṃ upasamanaina upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa vainaiyyānaṃ āsayādiparijānanaina pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇanti| tadapi asiddhaṃ, apariyausitattā buddhakic‍cassa|

catutthai ṭhānai parinibbānai caturadhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇanti aparai| tatra hi parinibbutattā paramatthasac‍casampattiyā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggaina cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| sabbasaṅkhārūpasamaina  upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ| pañ‍ñāpayaujanapariniṭṭhānaina pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇanti| tatra mahāpurisassa visaisaina maittākhaittai abhijātiyaṃ sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇamabhibyattaṃ, visaisaina karuṇākhaittai abhisambaudhiyaṃ pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇamabhibyattaṃ, visaisaina muditākhaittai dhammacak‍kappavattanai cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa cāgādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇamabhibyattaṃ, visaisaina upaikkhākhaittai parinibbānai upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa upasamādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇamabhibyattanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

tatra  sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa saṃvāsaina sīlaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa saṃvauhāraina saucaiyyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa āpadāsu thāmau vaiditabbau| pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa sākacchāya pañ‍ñā vaiditabbā| aivaṃ sīlājīvacittadiṭṭhivisuddhiyau vaiditabbā| tathā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina dausāgatiṃ na gacchati avisaṃvādanatau| cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina laubhāgatiṃ na gacchati anabhisaṅgatau| upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina bhayāgatiṃ na gacchati anaparādhatau| pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina mauhāgatiṃ na gacchati yathā bhūtāvabaudhatau|

tathā paṭhamaina aduṭṭhau adhivāsaiti, dutiyaina aluddhau paṭisaivati, tatiyaina abhītau parivaj‍jaiti, catutthaina asammūḷhau vinaudaiti| paṭhamaina naikkhammasukhappatti, itaraihi pavivaikaupasamasambaudhisukhappattiyau hauntīti daṭṭhabbā| tathā vivaikajapītisukhasamādhijapītisukhaapītijakāyasukhasatipārisuddhija- upaikkhāsukhappattiyau aitaihi catūhi yathāk‍kamaṃ haunti| aivamanaikaguṇānubandhaihi  catūhi adhiṭṭhānaihi sabbapāramisamūhasaṅgahau vaiditabbau| yathā ca catūhi adhiṭṭhānaihi sabbapāramisaṅgahau, aivaṃ karuṇāpañ‍ñāhipīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| sabbaupi hi baudhisambhārau karuṇāpañ‍ñāhi saṅgahitau| karuṇāpañ‍ñāpariggahitā hi dānādiguṇā mahābaudhisambhārā bhavanti buddhattasiddhipariyausānāti| aivamaitāsaṃ saṅgahau vaiditabbau|

kau sampādanūpāyauti? sakalassāpi puñ‍ñādisambhārassa sammāsambaudhiṃ uddissa anavasaisasambharaṇaṃ avaikal‍lakāritāyaugaina, tattha ca sak‍kac‍cakāritā ādarabahumānayaugaina, sātac‍cakāritā nirantarayaugaina, cirakālādiyaugau ca antarā avausānāpaj‍janaināti| taṃ panassa kālaparimāṇaṃ paratau āvi bhavissati| iti caturaṅgayaugau aitāsaṃ pāramīnaṃ sampādanūpāyau| tathā mahāsattaina baudhāya paṭipaj‍jantaina sammāsambaudhāya buddhānaṃ puraitaramaiva attā  niyyātaitabbau – ‘‘imāhaṃ attabhāvaṃ buddhānaṃ niyyātaimī’’ti| taṃtaṃpariggahavatthuñ‍ca paṭilābhatau puraitaramaiva dānamukhai nissaj‍jitabbaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci mayhaṃ uppaj‍janakaṃ jīvitaparikkhārajātaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ sati yācakai dassāmi, taisaṃ pana din‍nāvasaisaṃ aiva mayā paribhuñ‍jitabba’’nti|

aivaṃ  hissa sammadaiva paric‍cāgāya katai cittābhisaṅkhārai yaṃ uppaj‍jati pariggahavatthu aviñ‍ñāṇakaṃ saviñ‍ñāṇakaṃ vā, tattha yai imai pubbai dānai akataparicayau, pariggahavatthussa parittabhāvau, uḷāramanuñ‍ñatā, parikkhayacintāti cattārau dānavinibandhā, taisu yadā mahābaudhisattassa saṃvij‍jamānaisu daiyyadhammaisu pac‍cupaṭṭhitai ca yācakajanai dānai cittaṃ na pakkhandati na kamati| taina niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ ‘‘addhāhaṃ dānai pubbai akataparicayau, taina mai aitarahi dātukamyatā cittai na saṇṭhātī’’ti| sau aivaṃ mai itau paraṃ dānābhirataṃ cittaṃ bhavissati, handāhaṃ itau paṭṭhāya dānaṃ dassāmi, nanu mayā paṭikac‍caiva pariggahavatthu yācakānaṃ paric‍cattanti dānaṃ daiti muttacāgau payatapāṇī vausaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| aivaṃ mahāsattassa paṭhamau dānavinibandhau hatau hauti vihatau samucchin‍nau|

tathā mahāsattau daiyyadhammassa parittabhāvai sati vaikal‍lai ca iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘‘ahaṃ khau pubbai adānasīlatāya aitarahi aivaṃ pac‍cayavikalau jātau, tasmā idāni mayā parittaina vā hīnaina vā yathāladdhaina daiyyadhammaina attānaṃ pīḷaitvāpi dānamaiva dātabbaṃ, yaināhaṃ āyatimpi dānapāramiṃ matthakaṃ pāpaissāmī’’ti| sau itarītaraina taṃ dānaṃ daiti muttacāgau payatapāṇī vausaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| aivaṃ mahāsattassa dutiyau dānavinibandhau hatau hauti vihatau samucchin‍nau|

tathā mahāsattau daiyyadhammassa uḷāramanuñ‍ñatāya adātukamyatācittai uppaj‍jamānai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘‘nanu tayā sappurisa uḷāratamā sabbasaiṭṭhā sammāsambaudhi abhipatthitā, tasmā tadatthaṃ tayā uḷāramanuñ‍ñai aiva daiyyadhammai dātuṃ yuttarūpa’’nti| sau uḷāraṃ manuñ‍ñañ‍ca daiti muttacāgau payatapāṇī vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| aivaṃ mahāpurisassa tatiyau dānavinibandhau hatau hauti vihatau samucchin‍nau|

tathā mahāsattau dānaṃ daintau yadā daiyyadhammassa parikkhayaṃ passati, sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘‘ayaṃ khau bhaugānaṃ sabhāvau, yadidaṃ khayadhammatā vayadhammatā ca| api ca  mai pubbai tādisassa dānassa akatattā aivaṃ bhaugānaṃ parikkhayau dissati, handāhaṃ yathāladdhaina daiyyadhammaina  parittaina vā vipulaina vā dānamaiva dadaiyyaṃ, yaināhaṃ āyatiṃ dānapāramiyā matthakaṃ pāpuṇissāmī’’ti| sau yathāladdhaina dānaṃ daiti muttacāgau payatapāṇī vausaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| aivaṃ mahāsattassa catutthau dānavinibandhau hatau hauti vihatau samucchin‍nau| aivaṃ yai yai dānapāramiyā vinibandhabhūtā anatthā, taisaṃ taisaṃ yathārahaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā paṭivinaudanaṃ upāyau| yathā ca dānapāramiyā, aivaṃ sīlapāramiādīsupi daṭṭhabbaṃ|

api ca yaṃ mahāsattassa buddhānaṃ attasan‍niyyātanaṃ, taṃ sammadaiva sabbapāramīnaṃ sampādanūpāyau| buddhānañhi attānaṃ niyyātaitvā ṭhitau mahāpurisau tattha tattha baudhisambhārapāripūriyā ghaṭaintau vāyamantau sarīrassa sukhūpakaraṇānañ‍ca upacchaidakaisu dussahaisupi kicchaisu durabhisambhavaisupi sattasaṅkhārasamupanītaisu anatthaisu tibbaisu pāṇaharaisu ‘‘ayaṃ mayā attabhāvau buddhānaṃ paric‍cattau, yaṃ vā taṃ vā aittha hautū’’ti taṃnimittaṃ na kampati na vaidhati, īsakampi añ‍ñathattaṃ na gacchati, kusalārambhai  añ‍ñadatthu acalādhiṭṭhānauva hauti, aivaṃ attasan‍niyyātanampi aitāsaṃ sampādanūpāyau|

api ca samāsatau katābhinīhārassa attani sinaihassa parisausanaṃ paraisu ca sinaihassa parivaḍḍhanaṃ aitāsaṃ sampādanūpāyau| sammāsambaudhisamadhigamāya hi katamahāpaṇidhānassa mahāsattassa yāthāvatau parijānanaina sabbaisu dhammaisu anupalittassa attani sinaihau parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchati, mahākaruṇāsamāsaivanaina pana piyaputtai viya sabbasattai sampassamānassa taisu maittākaruṇāsinaihau parivaḍḍhati, tatau ca taṃtadavatthānurūpaṃ attaparasantānaisu laubhadausamauhavigamaina vidūrīkatamacchariyādibaudhisambhārapaṭipakkhau mahāpurisau dānapiyavacanaatthacariyāsamānattatāsaṅkhātaihi catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi caturadhiṭṭhānānugataihi ac‍cantaṃ janassa saṅgahakaraṇaina upari yānattayai avatāraṇaṃ paripācanañ‍ca karauti|

mahāsattānañhi mahākaruṇā mahāpañ‍ñā ca dānaina alaṅkatā, dānaṃ piyavacanaina, piyavacanaṃ atthacariyāya, atthacariyā samānattatāya alaṅkatā  saṅgahitā ca| taisaṃ sabbaipi sattai attanā nibbisaisai katvā baudhisambhāraisu paṭipaj‍jantānaṃ sabbattha samānasukhadukkhatāya samānattatāya siddhi| buddhabhūtānampi ca taihaiva catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi caturadhiṭṭhānaparipūritābhivuddhaihi janassa ac‍cantikasaṅgahakaraṇaina abhivinayanaṃ sijjhati| dānañhi  sammāsambuddhānaṃ cāgādhiṭṭhānaina paripūritābhivuddhaṃ, piyavacanaṃ sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaina, atthacariyā pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaina, samānattatā upasamādhiṭṭhānaina paripūritābhivuddhā| tathāgatānañhi sabbasāvakapac‍caikabuddhaihi samānattatā parinibbānai| tatra hi naisaṃ avisaisatau aikībhāvau| tainaivāha ‘‘natthi vimuttiyā nānatta’’nti| haunti caittha –

‘‘sac‍cau cāgī upasantau, pañ‍ñavā anukampakau|

sambhatasabbasambhārau, kaṃ nāmatthaṃ na sādhayai||

‘‘mahākāruṇikau satthā, hitaisī ca upaikkhakau|

nirapaikkhau ca sabbattha, ahau acchariyau jinau||

‘‘virattau sabbadhammaisu, sattaisu ca upaikkhakau|

sadā  sattahitai yuttau, ahau acchariyau jinau||

‘‘sabbadā sabbasattānaṃ, hitāya ca sukhāya ca|

uyyuttau akilāsū ca, ahau acchariyau jinau’’ti||

kittakaina kālaina sampādananti? haiṭṭhimaina tāva paricchaidaina cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni mahākappānaṃ satasahassañ‍ca, majjhimaina aṭṭha asaṅkhyaiyyāni mahākappānaṃ satasahassañ‍ca, uparimaina pana sauḷasa asaṅkhyaiyyāni mahākappānaṃ satasahassañ‍ca| aitai ca bhaidā yathāk‍kamaṃ pañ‍ñādhikasaddhādhikavīriyādhikavasaina ñātabbā| pañ‍ñādhikānañhi saddhā mandā hauti pañ‍ñā tikkhā, saddhādhikānaṃ pañ‍ñā majjhimā hauti, vīriyādhikānaṃ pañ‍ñā mandā, pañ‍ñānubhāvaina ca sammāsambaudhi adhigantabbāti aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaṃ|

aparai pana ‘‘vīriyassa tikkhamajjhimamudubhāvaina baudhisattānaṃ ayaṃ kālavibhāgau’’ti vadanti| avisaisaina pana vimuttiparipācanīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ tikkhamajjhimamudubhāvaina yathāvuttakālabhaidaina baudhisambhārā taisaṃ pāripūriṃ gacchantīti tayaupaitai kālabhaidā yuttātipi vadanti| aivaṃ tividhā  hi baudhisattā abhinīhārakkhaṇai bhavanti ugghahaṭitañ‍ñūvipañ‍citañ‍ñūnaiyyabhaidaina| taisu yau ugghaṭitañ‍ñū, sau sammāsambuddhassa sammukhā catuppadikaṃ gāthaṃ suṇantau gāthāya tatiyapadai apariyausitai aiva chahi abhiñ‍ñāhi saha paṭisambhidāhi  arahattaṃ adhigantuṃ samatthūpanissayau hauti, sacai sāvakabaudhiyaṃ adhimuttau siyā|

dutiyau bhagavatau sammukhā catuppadikaṃ gāthaṃ suṇantau apariyausitai aiva gāthāya catutthapadai chahi abhiñ‍ñāhi arahattaṃ adhigantuṃ samatthūpanissayau hauti, yadi sāvakabaudhiyaṃ adhimuttau siyā|

itarau pana bhagavatau sammukhā catuppadikaṃ gāthaṃ sutvā pariyausitāya gāthāya chahi abhiñ‍ñāhi arahattaṃ pattuṃ samatthūpanissayau hauti|

tayaupaitai vinā kālabhaidaina katābhinīhārau buddhānaṃ santikai laddhabyākaraṇā ca anuk‍kamaina pāramiyau paripūraintā yathāk‍kamaṃ yathāvuttabhaidaina kālaina sammāsambaudhiṃ pāpuṇanti| taisu taisu pana kālabhaidaisu aparipuṇṇaisu tai  tai mahāsattā divasai divasai vaissantaradānasadisaṃ mahādānaṃ daintāpi tadanurūpai sīlādisabbapāramidhammai ācinantāpi pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajantāpi ñātatthacariyā laukatthacariyā buddhatthacariyā paramakauṭiṃ pāpaintāpi antarā ca sammāsambuddhā bhavissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| kasmā? ñāṇassa aparipac‍canatau buddhakārakadhammānaṃ apariniṭṭhānatau| paricchin‍nakālanipphāditaṃ viya hi sassaṃ yathāvuttakālaparicchaidaina parinipphāditā sammāsambaudhi tadanantarā sabbussāhaina vāyamantaināpi na sak‍kā adhigantunti pāramipāripūri yathāvuttakālavisaisaina sampaj‍jatīti vaiditabbaṃ|

kau ānisaṃsauti? yai tai katābhinīhārānaṃ baudhisattānaṃ –

‘‘aivaṃ sabbaṅgasampan‍nā, baudhiyā niyatā narā|

saṃsaraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ, kappakauṭisataihipi||

‘‘avīcimhi nuppaj‍janti, tathā laukantaraisu ca|

nijjhāmataṇhā khuppipāsā, na haunti kālakañ‍jikā||

‘‘na  haunti khuddakā pāṇā, upapaj‍jantāpi duggatiṃ|

jāyamānā manussaisu, jac‍candhā na bhavanti tai||

‘‘sautavaikal‍latā  natthi, na bhavanti mūgapakkhikā|

itthibhāvaṃ na gacchanti, ubhataubyañ‍janapaṇḍakā||

‘‘na bhavanti pariyāpan‍nā, baudhiyā niyatā narā|

muttā ānantarikaihi, sabbattha suddhagaucarā||

‘‘micchādiṭṭhiṃ na saivanti, kammakiriyadassanā|

vasamānāpi saggaisu, asañ‍ñaṃ nūpapaj‍jarai||

‘‘suddhāvāsaisu daivaisu, haitu nāma na vij‍jati|

naikkhammanin‍nā sappurisā, visaṃyuttā bhavābhavai|

caranti laukatthacariyāyau, pūrainti sabbapāramī’’ti|| (dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā; jā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.dūrainidānakathā; apa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.dūrainidānakathā) –

aivaṃ saṃvaṇṇitā ānisaṃsā| yai ca ‘‘satau sampajānau, ānanda, baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamatī’’tiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 3.200; dī॰ ni॰ 2.17) sauḷasa acchariyabbhutadhammappakārā, yai ca ‘‘sītaṃ byapagataṃ hauti, uṇhañ‍ca upasammatī’’tiādinā (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.83) ‘‘jāyamānai khau, sāriputta, baudhisattai ayaṃ dasasahassī laukadhātu saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhatī’’tiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 3.201; dī॰ ni॰ 2.32) ca dvattiṃsa pubbanimittappakārā, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi  baudhisattānaṃ adhippāyasamijjhanaṃ kammādīsu vasībhāvauti aivamādayau tattha tattha jātakabuddhavaṃsādīsu dassitākārā ānisaṃsā, tai sabbaipi aitāsaṃ ānisaṃsā| tathā yathānidassitabhaidā alaubhādausādiguṇayugalādayau cāti vaiditabbā|

api ca yasmā baudhisattau abhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya sabbasattānaṃ pitusamau hauti hitaisitāya, dakkhiṇaiyyakau garu bhāvanīyau paramañ‍ca puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ hauti guṇavisaisayaugaina| yaibhuyyaina ca manussānaṃ piyau hauti, amanussānaṃ piyau hauti, daivatāhi anupālīyati, maittākaruṇāparibhāvitasantānatāya vāḷamigādīhi ca anabhibhavanīyau hauti, yasmiṃ yasmiñ‍ca sattanikāyai  pac‍cājāyati, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ uḷāraina vaṇṇaina uḷāraina yasaina uḷāraina sukhaina uḷāraina balaina uḷāraina  ādhipataiyyaina añ‍ñai sattai abhibhavati puñ‍ñavisaisayaugatau|

appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau, suvisuddhā cassa saddhā hauti suvisadā, suvisuddhaṃ vīriyaṃ, satisamādhipañ‍ñā suvisadā, mandakilaisau hauti mandadarathau mandapariḷāhau, kilaisānaṃ mandabhāvainaiva suvacau hauti padakkhiṇaggāhī, khamau hauti sauratau, sakhilau hauti paṭisanthārakusalau, ak‍kaudhanau hauti anupanāhī, amakkhī hauti apaḷāsī, anissukī hauti amaccharī, asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī, athaddhau hauti anatimānī, asāraddhau hauti appamattau, paratau upatāpasahau hauti paraisaṃ anupatāpī, yasmiñ‍ca gāmakhaittai paṭivasati, tattha sattānaṃ bhayādayau upaddavā yaibhuyyaina anuppan‍nā nuppaj‍janti, uppan‍nā ca vūpasammanti, yaisu ca apāyaisu uppaj‍jati, na tattha pacurajanau viya dukkhaina adhimattaṃ pīḷīyati, bhiyyausaumattāya saṃvaigamāpaj‍jati| tasmā mahāpurisassa yathārahaṃ tasmiṃ tasmiṃ bhavai labbhamānā aitai sattānaṃ pitusamatādakkhiṇaiyyatādayau guṇavisaisā ānisaṃsāti vaiditabbā|

tathā āyusampadā rūpasampadā kulasampadā issariyasampadā ādaiyyavacanatā mahānubhāvatāti aitaipi mahāpurisassa pāramīnaṃ ānisaṃsāti vaiditabbā| tattha āyusampadā nāma tassaṃ tassaṃ upapattiyaṃ dīghāyukatā ciraṭṭhitikatā, tāya yathāraddhāni kusalasamādānāni pariyausāpaiti, bahuñ‍ca kusalaṃ upacinauti| rūpasampadā nāma  abhirūpatā dassanīyatā pāsādikatā, tāya rūpappamāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ pasādāvahau hauti sambhāvanīyau| kulasampadā nāma uḷāraisu kulaisu abhinibbatti, tāya jātimadādimadamattānampi upasaṅkamanīyau hauti payirupāsanīyau, taina tai nibbisaivanai karauti| issariyasampadā nāma mahāvibhavatā mahaisakkhatā mahāparivāratā ca, tāhi saṅgaṇhitabbai catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi saṅgaṇhituṃ, niggahaitabbai dhammaina niggahaituñ‍ca samatthau hauti|

ādaiyyavacanatā nāma saddhaiyyatā pac‍cayikatā, tāya sattānaṃ pamāṇabhūtau hauti, alaṅghanīyā cassa āṇā hauti| mahānubhāvatā nāma ānubhāvamahantatā, tāya paraihi na abhibhūyati, sayamaiva  pana parai añ‍ñadatthu abhibhavati dhammaina samaina yathābhūtaguṇaihi ca, aivamaitai āyusampadādayau mahāpurisassa pāramīnaṃ ānisaṃsā, sayañ‍ca aparimāṇassa puñ‍ñasambhārassa parivuḍḍhihaitubhūtā yānattayai sattānaṃ avatāraṇassa paripācanassa ca kāraṇabhūtāti vaiditabbā|

kiṃ  phalanti? samāsatau tāva sammāsambuddhabhāvau aitāsaṃ phalaṃ, vitthāratau pana dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaasītianubyañ‍janabyāmappabhādianaikaguṇagaṇasamuj‍jalarūpakāyasampatti- adhiṭṭhānasabalacatuvaisāraj‍jachaasādhāraṇañāṇaaṭṭhārasāvaiṇika- buddhadhammappabhutianantāparimāṇa guṇasamudayaupasaubhinī dhammakāyasirī| yāvatā pana buddhaguṇā yai anaikaihipi kappaihi sammāsambuddhaināpi vācāya pariyausāpaituṃ na sak‍kā, idamaitāsaṃ phalaṃ| vuttañ‍caitaṃ –

‘‘buddhaupi buddhassa bhaṇaiyya vaṇṇaṃ, kappampi cai añ‍ñamabhāsamānau|

khīyaitha kappau ciradīghamantarai, vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassā’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.304; 3.141; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.425; udā॰ aṭṭha॰ 53; cariyā॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā)|

aivamaittha pāramīsu pakiṇṇakakathā vaiditabbā|

yaṃ pana pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘datvā dātabbakaṃ dāna’’ntiādinā sabbāpi pāramī aikajjhaṃ dassaitvā paratau ‘‘kausaj‍jaṃ bhayatau disvā’’tiādinā pariyausānagāthādvayaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ  yaihi vīriyārambhamaittābhāvanā appamādavihāraihi yathāvuttā buddhakārakadhammā visadabhāvaṃ gatā sammāsambaudhisaṅkhātā ca attanau vimutti paripācitā, taihi vainaiyyānampi vimuttiparipācanāya auvādadānatthaṃ vutthaṃ|

tattha kausaj‍jaṃ bhayatau disvā, vīriyārambhañ‍ca khaimatauti iminā paṭipakkhai ādīnavadassanamukhaina vīriyārambhai ānisaṃsaṃ dassaiti| āraddhavīriyā hauthāti iminā vīriyārambhai niyaujaiti| yasmā ca –

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudapanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 183; dī॰ ni॰ 2.90; naitti॰ 30, 50) –

saṅkhaipatau| vitthāratau pana sakalaina buddhavacanaina pakāsitā sabbāpi sampattiyau aikantainaiva sammappadhānādhīnā, tasmā bhagavā vīriyārambhai niyaujaitvā ‘‘aisā buddhānusāsanī’’ti āha|

tatrāyaṃ  saṅkhaipatthau – yvāyaṃ sabbasaṃkilaisamūlabhāvatau sabbānatthavidhāyakanti kausaj‍jaṃ bhayatau tappaṭipakkhatau catūhi yaugaihi anupaddavabhāvasādhanatau vīriyārambhañ‍ca khaimatau disvā adhisīlasikkhādisampādanavasaina vīriyassa ārambhau sammappadhānānuyaugau, tattha yaṃ sammadaiva niyaujana, ‘‘āraddhavīriyā hauthā’’ti, aisā buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ anusāsanī anusiṭṭhi auvādauti| saisagāthāsupi imināva nayaina atthau vaiditabbau|

ayaṃ pana visaisau – vivādanti viruddhavādaṃ, chavivādavatthuvasaina vivadananti atthau| avivādanti vivādapaṭipakkhaṃ maittāvacīkammaṃ, maittābhāvanaṃ vā| atha vā avivādanti avivādahaitubhūtaṃ chabbidhaṃ sāraṇīyadhammaṃ| samaggāti avaggā, kāyaina caiva cittaina ca sahitā aviramitā aviyuttāti atthau| sakhilāti sak‍kīlā mudusīlā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi muduhadayāti atthau| aisā buddhānusāsanīti aittha sabbaina sabbaṃ vivādamanupagamma yadidaṃ chasāraṇīyadhammaparipūraṇavasaina samaggavāsai niyaujanaṃ, aisā buddhānaṃ anusiṭṭhīti yaujaitabbaṃ| samaggavāsañhi vasamānā sīladiṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatā avivadamānā sukhainaiva tissau sikkhā paripūraissantīti satthā samaggavāsai niyaujanaṃ attanau sāsananti dassaisi|

pamādanti pamaj‍janaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pamussanaṃ akusalaisu ca dhammaisu cittavaussaggaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘tattha katamau pamādau, kāyaduc‍caritai  vā vacīduc‍caritai vā manauduc‍caritai vā pañ‍casu vā kāmaguṇaisu cittassa vausaggau vausaggānuppadānaṃ kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ bhāvanāya asak‍kac‍cakiriyatā, asātac‍cakiriyatā, anaṭṭhitakiriyatā, aulīnavuttitā, nikkhittachandatā, nikkhittadhuratā anāsaivanā abhāvanā abahulīkammaṃ…pai॰… yau aivarūpau pamādau pamaj‍janā pamaj‍jitattaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati pamādau’’ti (vibha॰ 846)|

appamādanti appamaj‍janaṃ| taṃ pamādassa paṭipakkhatau vaiditabbaṃ| atthatau hi appamādau nāma satiyā avippavāsau, ‘‘satiyā avippavāsau’’ti ca  nic‍caṃ upaṭṭhitāya satiyā aivaitaṃ nāmaṃ| aparai pana ‘‘satisampajañ‍ñappadhānā tathā pavattā cattārau arūpinau khandhā appamādau’’ti vadanti| yasmā pana appamādabhāvanā nāma visuṃ aikā bhāvanā natthi| yā hi kāci puñ‍ñakiriyā kusalakiriyā, sabbā sā appamādabhāvanātvaiva vaiditabbā|

visaisatau pana vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ saraṇagamanaṃ kāyikavācasikasaṃvarañ‍ca upādāya sabbā sīlabhāvanā, sabbā samādhibhāvanā, sabbā pañ‍ñābhāvanā, sabbā kusalabhāvanā, anavaj‍jabhāvanā, appamādabhāvanā| ‘‘appamādau’’ti hi idaṃ padaṃ mahantaṃ atthaṃ dīpaiti, mahantaṃ atthaṃ pariggahaitvā tiṭṭhati, sakalampi taipiṭakaṃ buddhavacanaṃ āharitvā appamādapadassa atthaṃ katvā kathaintau dhammakathikau ‘‘atitthaina pakkhandau’’ti na vattabbau| kasmā? appamādapadassa mahantabhāvatau| tathā hi sammāsambuddhau kusinārāyaṃ yamakasālānamantarai parinibbānasamayai nipan‍nau abhisambaudhitau paṭṭhāya pañ‍cacattālīsāya vassaisu attanā bhāsitaṃ dhammaṃ aikainaiva padaina saṅgahaitvā dassaintau ‘‘appamādaina sampādaithā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.218) bhikkhūnaṃ auvādamadāsi| tathā cāha – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici jaṅgamānaṃ pāṇānaṃ padajātāni, sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati yadidaṃ mahantattaina, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā, appamādau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.300 thaukaṃ visadisaṃ)| tattha appamādabhāvanaṃ sikhāppattaṃ dassaintau satthā ‘‘bhāvaithaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ magga’’nti āha|

tassatthau – yau aisa sīlādikhandhattayasaṅgahau sammādiṭṭhipubbaṅgamau sammādiṭṭhiādīnaṃyaiva  aṭṭhan‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ vasaina aṭṭhaṅgikau ariyamaggau, taṃ bhāvaitha attanau santānai uppādaitha| dassanamaggamattai aṭhatvā upari tiṇṇaṃ maggānaṃ uppādanavasaina vaḍḍhaitha, aivaṃ vau appamādabhāvanā sikhāppattā bhavissatīti| aisā buddhānusāsanīti yadidaṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu appamaj‍janaṃ, tañ‍ca ussuk‍kāpaitvā ariyamaggassa bhāvanā aisā buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ anusiṭṭhi auvādauti|

iti  bhagavā arahattanikūṭainaiva cariyāpiṭakadaisanaṃ niṭṭhāpaisi| itthaṃ sudantiādīsu itthanti kappai ca satasahassaitiādinā (cariyā॰ 1.1) pakāraina| sudanti nipātamattaṃ| bhagavāti bhāgyavantatādīhi kāraṇaihi bhagavā| attanau pubbacariyanti purimāsu akittipaṇḍitādijātīsu attanau paṭipattiduk‍karakiriyaṃ| sambhāvayamānauti hatthatalai āmalakaṃ viya sammadaiva pakāsaintau| buddhāpadāniyaṃ nāmāti buddhānaṃ purātanakammaṃ paurāṇaṃ duk‍karakiriyaṃ adhikic‍ca pavattattā daisitattā buddhāpadāniyanti aivaṃnāmakaṃ| dhammapariyāyanti dhammadaisanaṃ dhammabhūtaṃ vā kāraṇaṃ| abhāsitthāti avauca| yaṃ panaittha na vuttaṃ, taṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayattā uttānatthattā ca na vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

nigamanakathā

aittāvatā  ca –

visuddhacaritau satthā, buddhicariyāya pāragū|

sabbacariyāsu kusalau, laukācariyau anuttarau||

yaṃ acchariyadhammānaṃ, sabbamacchariyātigau|

attanau pubbacariyānaṃ, ānubhāvavibhāvanaṃ||

daisaisi nāthau cariyā-piṭakaṃ yañ‍ca tādinau|

dhammasaṅgāhakā thairā, saṅgāyiṃsu tathaiva ca||

tassa atthaṃ pakāsaituṃ, paurāṇaṭṭhakathānayaṃ|

nissāya yā samāraddhā, atthasaṃvaṇṇanā mayā||

yā tattha paramatthānaṃ, niddhāraitvā yathārahaṃ|

pakāsanā paramattha-dīpanī nāma nāmatau||

sampattā pariniṭṭhānaṃ, anākulavinicchayā|

sādhikāyaṭṭhavīsāya, pāḷiyā bhāṇavāratau||

iti taṃ saṅkharauntaina, yaṃ taṃ adhigataṃ mayā|

puñ‍ñaṃ tassānubhāvaina, laukanāthassa sāsanaṃ||

augāhaitvā  visuddhāya, sīlādipaṭipattiyā|

sabbaipi daihinau hauntu, vimuttirasabhāginau||

ciraṃ  tiṭṭhatu laukasmiṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanaṃ|

tasmiṃ sagāravā nic‍caṃ, hauntu sabbaipi pāṇinau||

sammā vassatu kālaina, daivaupi jagatīpati|

saddhammaniratau laukaṃ, dhammainaiva pasāsatūti||

iti badaratitthavihāravāsinā ācariyadhammapālaina

katā

cariyāpiṭakavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|
